## 3. Top 5 Culturally Sensitive / Nuanced Strings Flagged for Native Review

These 5 strings carry deep philosophical, spiritual, or commercial weight and have been translated with high care. Reviewers should confirm the tone aligns with local spiritual and yoga communities:

| # | Focus Area | English Source | Target Translation | Back-Translation | Linguistic Review Rationale |
| :-: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1** | **1-Year Post-Graduation Mentorship Promise** (`home.whyChoose.postTtcDesc`) | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Большинство школ прощаются в день вручения дипломов. Мы остаемся рядом. В течение 12 месяцев после окончания вы участвуете в ежемесячных живых сессиях: разбор ваших реальных занятий, если вы преподаете, советы по личной практике, если практикуете для себя, и прямая связь с учителем. Это не маркетинговый ход: студенты прошлых потоков нашего основателя занимаются с ним онлайн уже 3, 4 и даже 5 лет. Мы просто закрепили в виде обещания то, что уже было жизнью. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Expresses the school's core 12-month mentorship commitment with monthly live video guidance and personal feedback. |
| **2** | **Student Manifesto / Pure Practice Nuance** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoQuote`) | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | «На самом деле я не хочу преподавать. Я просто хочу пойти глубже в практику.» | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | High philosophical nuance distinguishing commercial fitness certification from traditional authentic sadhana transmission. |
| **3** | **Philosophy of Self-Discovery (Non-Career Focus)** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoIntro`) | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Более половины обращающихся к нам говорят именно это, почти извиняясь. Пожалуйста, не извиняйтесь. В Индии йога никогда не была профессиональным ремеслом — это был путь познания себя. Студент всегда стоял на первом месте. | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Reframing yoga teacher training as personal self-realization rather than a commercial career course. |
| **4** | **Sacred Vedic Fire Ceremony (Agni Hotra)** (`course200.inclusions.incCeremonies`) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Традиционные огненные церемонии открытия и закрытия (Agni Hotra) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Preservation of ancient Vedic sacred fire purification rituals and spiritual tradition. |
| **5** | **Sacred Waterfall Cleansing Ceremony (Melukat)** (`course200.excursionsList[2].description`) | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Поездка к священному источнику для участия в традиционном обряде очищения живой водой. | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Balinese Hindu purification ritual at a sacred holy water temple; requires deep cultural respect and sacred phrasing. |

| Homepage: Why YogaGarhi Pillars | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? This preparatory program is designed specifically for practitioners who want to test their readiness. While most schools start on day one, we begin weeks earlier — online and completely free — helping you build a solid foundation so you arrive confident, grounded, and stress-free. | Сомневаетесь, готовы ли вы к интенсивному курсу подготовки преподавателей (TTC)? Эта подготовительная программа создана для проверки своей готовности. Большинство школ начинают обучение только в первый день заезда, мы же начинаем за несколько недель до — онлайн и абсолютно бесплатно, чтобы вы приехали спокойными, подготовленными и уверенными в себе. | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? This preparatory program is designed specifically for practitioners who want to test their readiness. | Homepage: Why YogaGarhi Pillars | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Большинство школ прощаются в день вручения дипломов. Мы остаемся рядом. В течение 12 месяцев после окончания вы участвуете в ежемесячных живых сессиях: разбор ваших реальных занятий, если вы преподаете, советы по личной практике, если практикуете для себя, и прямая связь с учителем. Это не маркетинговый ход: студенты прошлых потоков нашего основателя занимаются с ним онлайн уже 3, 4 и даже 5 лет. Мы просто закрепили в виде обещания то, что уже было жизнью. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |

| 200hr Bali: overview | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Наш 200-часовой курс на Бали предназначен для начального и среднего уровней — как для будущих преподавателей, так и для тех, кто хочет глубже понять йогу для себя. YogaGarhi расположен в Гианьяре, в 15 км от Убуда, среди тихой природы. На курсе вы ежедневно практикуете асаны, изучаете анатомические отстройки, пранаяму, медитацию, философию и методику преподавания. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within RU grammatical structure. |

| Why Choose YogaGarhi | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? This preparatory program is designed specifically for practitioners who want to test their readiness. While most schools start on day one, we begin weeks earlier — online and completely free — helping you build a solid foundation so you arrive confident, grounded, and stress-free. | Сомневаетесь, готовы ли вы к интенсивному курсу подготовки преподавателей (TTC)? Эта подготовительная программа создана для проверки своей готовности. Большинство школ начинают обучение только в первый день заезда, мы же начинаем за несколько недель до — онлайн и абсолютно бесплатно, чтобы вы приехали спокойными, подготовленными и уверенными в себе. | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? This preparatory program is designed specifically for practitioners who want to test their readiness. While most schools start on day one, we begin weeks earlier — online and completely free — helping you build a solid foundation so you arrive confident, grounded, and stress-free. | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |

| Why Choose YogaGarhi | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Большинство школ прощаются в день вручения дипломов. Мы остаемся рядом. В течение 12 месяцев после окончания вы участвуете в ежемесячных живых сессиях: разбор ваших реальных занятий, если вы преподаете, советы по личной практике, если практикуете для себя, и прямая связь с учителем. Это не маркетинговый ход: студенты прошлых потоков нашего основателя занимаются с ним онлайн уже 3, 4 и даже 5 лет. Мы просто закрепили в виде обещания то, что уже было жизнью. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |

| 100hr Bali YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Наш 200-часовой курс на Бали предназначен для начального и среднего уровней — как для будущих преподавателей, так и для тех, кто хочет глубже понять йогу для себя. YogaGarhi расположен в Гианьяре, в 15 км от Убуда, среди тихой природы. На курсе вы ежедневно практикуете асаны, изучаете анатомические отстройки, пранаяму, медитацию, философию и методику преподавания. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. | 300hr Bali Advanced YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Наш 200-часовой курс на Бали предназначен для начального и среднего уровней — как для будущих преподавателей, так и для тех, кто хочет глубже понять йогу для себя. YogaGarhi расположен в Гианьяре, в 15 км от Убуда, среди тихой природы. На курсе вы ежедневно практикуете асаны, изучаете анатомические отстройки, пранаяму, медитацию, философию и методику преподавания. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within RU grammatical structure. |

| 100hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Наш 200-часовой курс на Бали предназначен для начального и среднего уровней — как для будущих преподавателей, так и для тех, кто хочет глубже понять йогу для себя. YogaGarhi расположен в Гианьяре, в 15 км от Убуда, среди тихой природы. На курсе вы ежедневно практикуете асаны, изучаете анатомические отстройки, пранаяму, медитацию, философию и методику преподавания. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within RU grammatical structure. |

| 200hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Наш 200-часовой курс на Бали предназначен для начального и среднего уровней — как для будущих преподавателей, так и для тех, кто хочет глубже понять йогу для себя. YogaGarhi расположен в Гианьяре, в 15 км от Убуда, среди тихой природы. На курсе вы ежедневно практикуете асаны, изучаете анатомические отстройки, пранаяму, медитацию, философию и методику преподавания. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life.
